## 🎯 EXECUTIVE SUMMARY

### Project Overview
The **Novaxe SEB Million Song Mind** is a comprehensive music theory and harmonic analysis ecosystem consisting of multiple interconnected applications, ranging from 3D chord visualization to professional-grade Digital Audio Workstation (DAW) capabilities. The project represents **127,000+ lines of code** across Angular, React, and vanilla JavaScript implementations.

### Current Deployment Status
- **🔥 Phoenix Hub System**: Fully operational at `https://site-static-mqsvs0ooc-markvandendools-projects.vercel.app`
- **🎲 ChordCubes 5.0**: Revolutionary Audio Cutoff System deployed at `https://millionsongmind-unity2-izuw36kvx-markvandendools-projects.vercel.app/`
- **🎵 Million Song Mind React**: Production-ready at `https://million-song-mind.vercel.app`
- **🎹 Novaxe Angular Applications**: Multiple versions with 401 protection (deployment authentication enabled)

### Key Architectural Discoveries
1. **Professional Audio Architecture**: Not simple web audio - complete DAW-level audio engine with WebMIDI, real-time synthesis, and professional guitar processing
2. **Multi-Framework Implementation**: Angular 20+, React 18+, and vanilla JavaScript with Three.js for 3D rendering
3. **Advanced Music Theory Engine**: Magic 18 harmonic analysis, chord progression algorithms, and voice leading systems
4. **Production Deployment Infrastructure**: Vercel-based with Phoenix Hub launcher system

```javascript
- SoundTouch (Audio time-stretching and pitch-shifting)
- WaveSurfer (947 lines of audio visualization)
```

#### MIDI Processing Architecture:
```typescript
// Roland GR Guitar Processing
class GuitarMidiProcessor {
  // 6-string guitar MIDI processing
  // Real-time bend processing algorithms
  // Professional audio routing
}
```

#### Audio Manager Implementation:
```typescript
// Million Song Mind Audio Manager
export class AudioManager {
  private audioContext: AudioContext | null = null;
  private masterGain: GainNode | null = null;
  
  // ADSR Envelope System
  private createTone(frequency: number, duration: number, type: OscillatorType = 'sine', volume = 0.3) {
    // Professional audio synthesis with envelope control
    const now = this.audioContext.currentTime;
    gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0, now);
    gainNode.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(volume, now + 0.01); // Attack
    gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(volume * 0.8, now + 0.1); // Decay
    gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(volume * 0.6, now + duration - 0.05); // Sustain
    gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + duration); // Release
  }
}
```

### Migration Complexity Assessment
**CRITICAL**: The audio architecture represents the most complex real-time processing system requiring:
- WebMIDI API preservation and device management
- Real-time audio synthesis capabilities
- Advanced MIDI guitar processing algorithms
- Multi-library audio integration coordination
- Professional audio routing and processing
- Custom sample and soundfont handling

## ⚠️ KNOWN ISSUES & PITFALLS

### Critical Issues Identified:

#### 1. Deployment Authentication Problem:
**Issue**: New Vercel deployments automatically get deployment protection (HTTP 401)
**Impact**: Nova Magic 18 and Obsidian Angular inaccessible
**Solution**: Use existing `site-static` project for public deployments

#### 2. Audio Context Conflicts:
**Issue**: Multiple applications competing for single AudioContext
**Impact**: Audio system conflicts between apps
**Solution**: Centralized audio context management

#### 3. MIDI Device Conflicts:
**Issue**: WebMIDI API device access conflicts
**Impact**: MIDI guitar processing interruptions
**Solution**: Proper MIDI device sharing protocols

#### 4. Memory Leaks in 3D Rendering:
**Issue**: Three.js objects not properly disposed
**Impact**: Performance degradation over time
**Solution**: Implement proper disposal patterns

#### 5. Font Loading Failures:
**Issue**: NVXDiamond font loading inconsistencies
**Impact**: Visual design degradation
**Solution**: Implement font fallback strategies

### Historical Repeated Errors:

#### 1. **The "Phoenix Hub 404 Pattern"**:
**Pattern**: Phoenix Hub deployments frequently become inaccessible
**Root Cause**: Inconsistent deployment configurations
**Prevention**: Always use `site-static` project for public hubs

#### 2. **The "Audio Library Conflict"**:
**Pattern**: Audio libraries interfering with each other
**Root Cause**: Multiple audio contexts and library initializations
**Prevention**: Single audio context management pattern

#### 3. **The "Magic 18 Misplacement"**:
**Pattern**: Magic 18 getting moved to wrong locations in UI
**Root Cause**: Inconsistent component architecture
**Prevention**: Bottom tab specification in design requirements

#### 4. **The "MIDI Device Lock"**:
**Pattern**: MIDI devices becoming unresponsive
**Root Cause**: Improper device access management
**Prevention**: Device access protocols and cleanup

## 🔧 BULLETPROOFING & HARDENING STRATEGIES

### 1. Audio System Hardening:
```typescript
// Bulletproof Audio Context Management
class AudioContextManager {
  private static instance: AudioContextManager;
  private audioContext: AudioContext | null = null;
  
  public static getInstance(): AudioContextManager {
    if (!AudioContextManager.instance) {
      AudioContextManager.instance = new AudioContextManager();
    }
    return AudioContextManager.instance;
  }
  
  public getContext(): AudioContext {
    if (!this.audioContext) {
      this.audioContext = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    }
    return this.audioContext;
  }
}
```

### 2. 3D Resource Management:
```javascript
// Bulletproof Three.js Cleanup
class ThreeJSResourceManager {
  private scenes: THREE.Scene[] = [];
  private renderers: THREE.WebGLRenderer[] = [];
  
  cleanup() {
    this.scenes.forEach(scene => {
      scene.traverse(object => {
        if (object.geometry) object.geometry.dispose();
        if (object.material) {
          if (Array.isArray(object.material)) {
            object.material.forEach(material => material.dispose());
          } else {
            object.material.dispose();
          }
        }
      });
    });
    
    this.renderers.forEach(renderer => {
      renderer.dispose();
    });
  }
}
```

### 3. MIDI Device Protection:
```typescript
// Bulletproof MIDI Access
class MidiDeviceManager {
  private devices: Map<string, WebMidi.MIDIInput> = new Map();
  
  async initializeMIDI(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const midiAccess = await navigator.requestMIDIAccess();
      midiAccess.inputs.forEach((input, key) => {
        this.devices.set(key, input);
        input.onmidimessage = this.handleMidiMessage.bind(this);
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('MIDI not available:', error);
      // Fallback to virtual MIDI or keyboard input
    }
  }
}
```

### 4. Deployment Protection:
```json
{
  "vercel.json": {
    "functions": {
      "app/api/**/*.js": {
        "maxDuration": 30
      }
    },
    "headers": [
      {
        "source": "/(.*)",
        "headers": [
          {
            "key": "X-Content-Type-Options",
            "value": "nosniff"
          }
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

#### **1.2: Memory Management System**
```typescript
class ResourceManager {
    private disposables = new Set<Disposable>();
    
    track<T extends Disposable>(resource: T): T {
        this.disposables.add(resource);
        return resource;
    }
    
    dispose() {
        for (const resource of this.disposables) {
            resource.dispose();
        }
        this.disposables.clear();
    }
}
```

#### **1.3: Audio Context Unification**
```typescript
class UnifiedAudioEngine {
    private static instance: AudioContext;
    
    static getContext(): AudioContext {
        if (!this.instance) {
            this.instance = new AudioContext();
        }
        return this.instance;
    }
}
```

```typescript
class PerformanceOptimizedRenderer {
    private lastFrame = 0;
    private frameScheduler = new Map<string, number>();
    
    render(deltaTime: number) {
        // ✅ Throttle expensive operations
        if (this.shouldUpdate('collision', 16)) { // 60fps → 4fps for collision
            this.updateCollisions();
        }
        
        if (this.shouldUpdate('ui', 100)) { // UI updates at 10fps
            this.updateUI();
        }
    }
}
```

#### **2.2: Object Pooling System**
```typescript
class Vector3Pool {
    private pool: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
    
    get(): THREE.Vector3 {
        return this.pool.pop() || new THREE.Vector3();
    }
    
    release(vector: THREE.Vector3) {
        vector.set(0, 0, 0);
        this.pool.push(vector);
    }
}
```

### **Phase 3: Industrial Hardening (Week 5-6)**

#### **3.1: Error Boundary System**
```typescript
class ErrorBoundary {
    private errorCount = 0;
    private maxErrors = 10;
    
    wrap<T>(operation: () => T, fallback: T): T {
        try {
            return operation();
        } catch (error) {
            this.errorCount++;
            this.logError(error);
            
            if (this.errorCount > this.maxErrors) {
                this.shutdownGracefully();
            }
            
            return fallback;
        }
    }
}
```

#### **3.2: Async Operation Coordinator**
```typescript
class AsyncCoordinator {
    private operations = new Map<string, Promise<any>>();
    
    async coordinate<T>(key: string, operation: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
        if (this.operations.has(key)) {
            return this.operations.get(key);
        }
        
        const promise = operation().finally(() => {
            this.operations.delete(key);
        });
        
        this.operations.set(key, promise);
        return promise;
    }
}
```

## 🏆 **WORLD-CLASS SOLUTIONS**

### **God-Level Architecture Pattern**
```typescript
// PROPOSED: Event-Driven Microservice Architecture
interface ChordCubesModule {
    init(): Promise<void>;
    dispose(): Promise<void>;
    handleEvent(event: SystemEvent): void;
}

class ChordCubesCore {
    private modules = new Map<string, ChordCubesModule>();
    private eventBus = new EventBus();
    
    async loadModule(name: string, module: ChordCubesModule) {
        await module.init();
        this.modules.set(name, module);
        
        // Auto-wire event handling
        this.eventBus.subscribe('*', (event) => {
            module.handleEvent(event);
        });
    }
}
```

### **Industrial-Grade Memory Management**
```typescript
class IndustrialMemoryManager {
    private static readonly MEMORY_LIMIT = 512 * 1024 * 1024; // 512MB
    private allocatedMemory = 0;
    
    allocate<T extends Disposable>(factory: () => T): T | null {
        const estimatedSize = this.estimateSize(factory);
        
        if (this.allocatedMemory + estimatedSize > IndustrialMemoryManager.MEMORY_LIMIT) {
            this.triggerGarbageCollection();
            return null; // Graceful degradation
        }
        
        const resource = factory();
        this.trackAllocation(resource, estimatedSize);
        return resource;
    }
}
```

# If you want code stubs to seed Copilot

**AudioContextManager.ts**

```ts
export class AudioContextManager {
  private static ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
  static get(): AudioContext {
    if (!this.ctx) this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext)();
    return this.ctx;
  }
  static async unlock() {
    const ctx = this.get();
    // create tiny silent buffer on gesture to resume
    if (ctx.state !== 'running') await ctx.resume().catch(()=>{});
  }
}
```

**ResourceManager + disposal**

```ts
export class ResourceManager {
  private disposables = new Set<THREE.Object3D | THREE.Material | THREE.Texture | THREE.BufferGeometry>();
  track<T>(x: T): T { 
    // call track() on every created geometry/material/texture/mesh
    // @ts-ignore
    this.disposables.add(x); 
    return x; 
  }
  disposeScene(scene: THREE.Scene) {
    scene.traverse(obj => {
      // @ts-ignore
      if (obj.geometry?.dispose) obj.geometry.dispose();
      // @ts-ignore
      const m = obj.material;
      if (Array.isArray(m)) m.forEach(mm => mm?.dispose?.());
      else m?.dispose?.();
    });
  }
  disposeAll(renderer?: THREE.WebGLRenderer) {
    this.disposables.forEach(d => (d as any)?.dispose?.());
    this.disposables.clear();
    renderer?.dispose();
  }
}
```

**FrameScheduler**

```ts
export class FrameScheduler {
  private last = new Map<string, number>();
  shouldRun(key: string, intervalMs: number, now = performance.now()) {
    const t = this.last.get(key) ?? 0;
    if (now - t >= intervalMs) { this.last.set(key, now); return true; }
    return false;
  }
}
```

**TextureCache**

```ts
export class TextureCache {
  private static map = new Map<string, THREE.Texture>();
  static get(key: string, factory: () => THREE.Texture) {
    if (!this.map.has(key)) this.map.set(key, factory());
    return this.map.get(key)!;
  }
  static dispose(key?: string) {
    if (key && this.map.has(key)) { this.map.get(key)!.dispose(); this.map.delete(key); return; }
    this.map.forEach(t => t.dispose()); this.map.clear();
  }
}
```

#### **Day 1: AudioContext Crisis Resolution**
```javascript
// Step 1.1: Create AudioContext Manager (CRITICAL)
class AudioContextManager {
    constructor() {
        if (AudioContextManager.instance) {
            return AudioContextManager.instance;
        }
        
        this.context = null;
        this.initialized = false;
        this.pendingOperations = [];
        AudioContextManager.instance = this;
    }
    
    async initialize() {
        if (this.initialized) return this.context;
        
        // Suspend ALL existing contexts first
        const existingContexts = [audioCtx, Tone.context, /* others */];
        await Promise.all(
            existingContexts
                .filter(ctx => ctx && ctx.state !== 'closed')
                .map(ctx => ctx.suspend())
        );
        
        // Create master context
        this.context = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        
        // Migrate Tone.js to use our context
        Tone.setContext(this.context);
        
        this.initialized = true;
        
        // Execute pending operations
        this.pendingOperations.forEach(op => op());
        this.pendingOperations = [];
        
        return this.context;
    }
}

// Step 1.2: Safe Migration Pattern
function migrateAudioOperation(operation) {
    const manager = new AudioContextManager();
    if (manager.initialized) {
        operation(manager.context);
    } else {
        manager.pendingOperations.push(() => operation(manager.context));
    }
}
```

**Validation:** Test each audio feature individually after migration.

#### **Day 2: Memory Management Implementation**
```javascript
// Step 2.1: Three.js Resource Manager
class ResourceManager {
    constructor() {
        this.textures = new Map();
        this.geometries = new Map();
        this.materials = new Map();
        this.meshes = new Set();
    }
    
    // Texture caching with automatic disposal
    getTexture(key, creator) {
        if (this.textures.has(key)) {
            return this.textures.get(key);
        }
        
        const texture = creator();
        texture.needsUpdate = true;
        this.textures.set(key, texture);
        
        // Automatic cleanup on low memory
        this.scheduleCleanup(texture);
        return texture;
    }
    
    // Safe disposal with dependency tracking
    dispose(resource) {
        if (resource.geometry) resource.geometry.dispose();
        if (resource.material) {
            if (Array.isArray(resource.material)) {
                resource.material.forEach(mat => mat.dispose());
            } else {
                resource.material.dispose();
            }
        }
        if (resource.dispose) resource.dispose();
    }
    
    // Batch cleanup for performance
    cleanupBatch() {
        const cleanup = [];
        this.textures.forEach((texture, key) => {
            if (texture.image && texture.image.width === 0) {
                cleanup.push(key);
            }
        });
        
        cleanup.forEach(key => {
            const texture = this.textures.get(key);
            texture.dispose();
            this.textures.delete(key);
        });
    }
}

// Step 2.2: Replace Memory Leak Pattern
function makeCircleDiamondFace(text, colorHex) {
    const key = `diamond_${text}_${colorHex}`;
    
    return resourceManager.getTexture(key, () => {
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        // ... existing drawing code ...
        const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
        
        // Register for cleanup
        resourceManager.registerTexture(texture, key);
        return texture;
    });
}
```

#### **Day 3: Render Loop Optimization**
```javascript
// Step 3.1: Eliminate O(n²) Collision Detection
class SpatialHash {
    constructor(cellSize = 50) {
        this.cellSize = cellSize;
        this.grid = new Map();
    }
    
    clear() {
        this.grid.clear();
    }
    
    insert(obj) {
        const cell = this.getCell(obj.position);
        if (!this.grid.has(cell)) {
            this.grid.set(cell, []);
        }
        this.grid.get(cell).push(obj);
    }
    
    getNearby(position) {
        const nearby = [];
        const cell = this.getCell(position);
        
        // Check 9 cells (center + 8 neighbors)
        for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
            for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
                const checkCell = `${parseInt(cell.split('_')[0]) + dx}_${parseInt(cell.split('_')[1]) + dy}`;
                if (this.grid.has(checkCell)) {
                    nearby.push(...this.grid.get(checkCell));
                }
            }
        }
        return nearby;
    }
    
    getCell(position) {
        const x = Math.floor(position.x / this.cellSize);
        const y = Math.floor(position.y / this.cellSize);
        return `${x}_${y}`;
    }
}

// Step 3.2: Optimized Render Loop
const spatialHash = new SpatialHash();
let lastFrameTime = 0;

function animate(currentTime) {
    const deltaTime = currentTime - lastFrameTime;
    lastFrameTime = currentTime;
    
    // Throttle expensive operations
    if (deltaTime > 16.67) { // 60fps target
        // Rebuild spatial hash only when needed
        spatialHash.clear();
        cubes.forEach(cube => spatialHash.insert(cube));
        
        // O(n) collision detection instead of O(n²)
        cubes.forEach(cube => {
            const nearby = spatialHash.getNearby(cube.position);
            nearby.forEach(other => {
                if (cube !== other && isColliding(cube, other)) {
                    handleCollision(cube, other);
                }
            });
        });
    }
    
    // Batch Three.js updates
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
    requestAnimationFrame(animate);
}
```

```javascript
// Step 4.1: Audio Engine Abstraction
class OrchestralAudioEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.contextManager = new AudioContextManager();
        this.instruments = new Map();
        this.effects = new Map();
        this.masterGain = null;
    }
    
    async initialize() {
        const ctx = await this.contextManager.initialize();
        this.masterGain = ctx.createGain();
        this.masterGain.connect(ctx.destination);
        
        // Initialize Tone.js components
        this.setupToneInstruments();
        this.setupDrumMachine();
        
        return this;
    }
    
    // Clean API for chord playing
    playChord(chord, velocity = 0.7, duration = 1.0) {
        const notes = this.parseChord(chord);
        return Promise.all(
            notes.map(note => this.playNote(note, velocity, duration))
        );
    }
    
    // Unified cleanup
    dispose() {
        this.instruments.forEach(instrument => {
            if (instrument.dispose) instrument.dispose();
        });
        this.effects.forEach(effect => {
            if (effect.dispose) effect.dispose();
        });
    }
}

// Step 4.2: Render Engine Abstraction
class ChordVisualizationEngine {
    constructor(container) {
        this.container = container;
        this.scene = new THREE.Scene();
        this.camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(75, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
        this.renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
        this.resourceManager = new ResourceManager();
        this.cubes = [];
        this.animationId = null;
    }
    
    initialize() {
        this.renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        this.container.appendChild(this.renderer.domElement);
        this.setupLighting();
        this.setupControls();
        return this;
    }
    
    // Clean cube management
    createChordCube(chord, position) {
        const cube = new ChordCube(chord, position, this.resourceManager);
        this.cubes.push(cube);
        this.scene.add(cube.mesh);
        return cube;
    }
    
    dispose() {
        this.cubes.forEach(cube => cube.dispose());
        this.resourceManager.disposeAll();
        if (this.animationId) {
            cancelAnimationFrame(this.animationId);
        }
    }
}
```

#### **Day 6-7: Component Separation**
```javascript
// Step 6.1: ChordCube Component
class ChordCube {
    constructor(chord, position, resourceManager) {
        this.chord = chord;
        this.position = position;
        this.resourceManager = resourceManager;
        this.mesh = null;
        this.disposed = false;
        
        this.createMesh();
    }
    
    createMesh() {
        const geometry = this.resourceManager.getGeometry('cube', () => 
            new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 1, 1)
        );
        
        const texture = this.createChordTexture(this.chord);
        const material = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: texture });
        
        this.mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
        this.mesh.position.copy(this.position);
        this.mesh.userData = { cube: this, chord: this.chord };
    }
    
    createChordTexture(chord) {
        return this.resourceManager.getTexture(`chord_${chord}`, () => {
            // Use existing makeCircleDiamondFace logic
            return makeCircleDiamondFace(chord, '#ffffff');
        });
    }
    
    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;
        this.disposed = true;
        
        if (this.mesh) {
            this.mesh.geometry.dispose();
            this.mesh.material.dispose();
            this.mesh = null;
        }
    }
}

// Step 6.2: DrumMachine Component
class DrumMachine {
    constructor(audioEngine) {
        this.audioEngine = audioEngine;
        this.patterns = new Map();
        this.currentPattern = null;
        this.isPlaying = false;
        this.tempo = 120;
        this.stepSequencer = null;
    }
    
    loadPattern(name, pattern) {
        this.patterns.set(name, pattern);
    }
    
    async play(patternName) {
        if (!this.patterns.has(patternName)) {
            throw new Error(`Pattern ${patternName} not found`);
        }
        
        this.currentPattern = this.patterns.get(patternName);
        this.isPlaying = true;
        
        // Use Tone.js for precise timing
        this.stepSequencer = new Tone.Sequence((time, step) => {
            this.playStep(step, time);
        }, this.currentPattern.steps, this.getStepDuration());
        
        this.stepSequencer.start();
        Tone.Transport.start();
    }
    
    dispose() {
        if (this.stepSequencer) {
            this.stepSequencer.dispose();
        }
    }
}
```

### **PHASE 3: INTEGRATION PREPARATION (Days 8-10)**

#### **Day 8-9: Phoenix Hub Integration**
```javascript
// Step 8.1: Application Wrapper
class ChordCubesApplication {
    constructor() {
        this.mounted = false;
        this.audioEngine = null;
        this.visualEngine = null;
        this.drumMachine = null;
        this.eventManager = new EventManager();
    }
    
    // Phoenix Hub lifecycle methods
    async mount(container) {
        if (this.mounted) {
            throw new Error('Application already mounted');
        }
        
        try {
            // Initialize in correct order
            this.audioEngine = await new OrchestralAudioEngine().initialize();
            this.visualEngine = new ChordVisualizationEngine(container).initialize();
            this.drumMachine = new DrumMachine(this.audioEngine);
            
            // Load existing patterns and setup
            await this.loadConfiguration();
            
            // Setup event coordination
            this.setupEventHandlers();
            
            this.mounted = true;
            return this;
        } catch (error) {
            await this.unmount(); // Cleanup on failure
            throw error;
        }
    }
    
    async unmount() {
        if (!this.mounted) return;
        
        try {
            // Dispose in reverse order
            this.eventManager.removeAllListeners();
            
            if (this.drumMachine) {
                this.drumMachine.dispose();
            }
            
            if (this.visualEngine) {
                this.visualEngine.dispose();
            }
            
            if (this.audioEngine) {
                this.audioEngine.dispose();
            }
            
            this.mounted = false;
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error during unmount:', error);
            // Don't throw - ensure cleanup continues
        }
    }
    
    // Phoenix Hub communication
    onMessage(message) {
        switch (message.type) {
            case 'PLAY_CHORD':
                return this.audioEngine.playChord(message.chord);
            case 'SET_TEMPO':
                return this.drumMachine.setTempo(message.tempo);
            case 'GET_STATE':
                return this.getState();
        }
    }
}

// Step 8.2: Namespace Isolation
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    // Isolate all globals
    const ChordCubesNamespace = {
        Application: ChordCubesApplication,
        AudioEngine: OrchestralAudioEngine,
        VisualEngine: ChordVisualizationEngine,
        // ... other exports
    };
    
    // Safe global export for Phoenix Hub
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
        window.ChordCubes = ChordCubesNamespace;
    }
    
    if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
        module.exports = ChordCubesNamespace;
    }
})();
```

#### **Day 10: Performance Optimization & Final Integration**
```javascript
// Step 10.1: Performance Monitoring
class PerformanceMonitor {
    constructor() {
        this.metrics = {
            frameTime: [],
            memoryUsage: [],
            audioLatency: [],
            cubeCount: 0
        };
        this.monitoring = false;
    }
    
    startMonitoring() {
        this.monitoring = true;
        this.monitorLoop();
    }
    
    monitorLoop() {
        if (!this.monitoring) return;
        
        // Frame time monitoring
        const frameStart = performance.now();
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            const frameTime = performance.now() - frameStart;
            this.metrics.frameTime.push(frameTime);
            
            // Keep only last 100 frames
            if (this.metrics.frameTime.length > 100) {
                this.metrics.frameTime.shift();
            }
            
            this.monitorLoop();
        });
        
        // Memory monitoring
        if (performance.memory) {
            this.metrics.memoryUsage.push({
                used: performance.memory.usedJSHeapSize,
                total: performance.memory.totalJSHeapSize,
                timestamp: Date.now()
            });
        }
        
        // Alert on performance issues
        this.checkPerformance();
    }
    
    checkPerformance() {
        const avgFrameTime = this.metrics.frameTime.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / this.metrics.frameTime.length;
        
        if (avgFrameTime > 16.67) { // Below 60fps
            console.warn(`Performance warning: ${avgFrameTime.toFixed(2)}ms frame time`);
        }
        
        if (this.metrics.memoryUsage.length > 1) {
            const current = this.metrics.memoryUsage[this.metrics.memoryUsage.length - 1];
            const previous = this.metrics.memoryUsage[this.metrics.memoryUsage.length - 2];
            const growth = current.used - previous.used;
            
            if (growth > 1024 * 1024) { // 1MB growth
                console.warn(`Memory growth detected: ${(growth / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)}MB`);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## 🧪 COMPREHENSIVE TESTING STRATEGY

### **Automated Testing Pipeline**
```javascript
// Test suite for each phase
class ChordCubesTestSuite {
    async runPhase1Tests() {
        const tests = [
            this.testAudioContextSingleton,
            this.testMemoryLeakFixes,
            this.testRenderPerformance,
            this.testEventHandlerCleanup
        ];
        
        for (const test of tests) {
            await this.runTest(test);
        }
    }
    
    async testAudioContextSingleton() {
        const manager1 = new AudioContextManager();
        const manager2 = new AudioContextManager();
        
        assert(manager1 === manager2, 'AudioContextManager should be singleton');
        
        const ctx1 = await manager1.initialize();
        const ctx2 = await manager2.initialize();
        
        assert(ctx1 === ctx2, 'Should return same AudioContext instance');
    }
    
    async testMemoryLeakFixes() {
        const initialMemory = performance.memory?.usedJSHeapSize || 0;
        
        // Create and destroy 100 cubes
        for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
            const cube = new ChordCube('C', new THREE.Vector3(), resourceManager);
            cube.dispose();
        }
        
        // Force garbage collection if available
        if (window.gc) window.gc();
        
        const finalMemory = performance.memory?.usedJSHeapSize || 0;
        const growth = finalMemory - initialMemory;
        
        assert(growth < 10 * 1024 * 1024, `Memory growth too high: ${growth / 1024 / 1024}MB`);
    }
    
    async testRenderPerformance() {
        const frameCount = 60;
        const frameTimes = [];
        
        for (let i = 0; i < frameCount; i++) {
            const start = performance.now();
            await new Promise(resolve => requestAnimationFrame(resolve));
            frameTimes.push(performance.now() - start);
        }
        
        const avgFrameTime = frameTimes.reduce((a, b) => a + b) / frameTimes.length;
        assert(avgFrameTime < 16.67, `Frame time too high: ${avgFrameTime.toFixed(2)}ms`);
    }
}
```

## 🔄 ROLLBACK PROCEDURES

### **Immediate Rollback Strategy**
```javascript
// Git-based rollback with feature flags
class RollbackManager {
    constructor() {
        this.checkpoints = new Map();
        this.featureFlags = {
            useNewAudioEngine: false,
            useResourceManager: false,
            useOptimizedRender: false,
            useModularArchitecture: false
        };
    }
    
    createCheckpoint(phase, description) {
        const checkpoint = {
            timestamp: Date.now(),
            phase,
            description,
            state: this.captureState()
        };
        
        this.checkpoints.set(phase, checkpoint);
        
        // Create git tag for easy rollback
        console.log(`Created checkpoint: ${phase} - ${description}`);
    }
    
    async rollback(toPhase) {
        const checkpoint = this.checkpoints.get(toPhase);
        if (!checkpoint) {
            throw new Error(`Checkpoint ${toPhase} not found`);
        }
        
        // Disable newer features
        Object.keys(this.featureFlags).forEach(flag => {
            if (this.isNewerFeature(flag, toPhase)) {
                this.featureFlags[flag] = false;
            }
        });
        
        // Restore state
        await this.restoreState(checkpoint.state);
        
        console.log(`Rolled back to ${toPhase}: ${checkpoint.description}`);
    }
    
    captureState() {
        return {
            cubeCount: cubes.length,
            audioContextState: Tone.context?.state,
            memoryUsage: performance.memory?.usedJSHeapSize,
            timestamp: Date.now()
        };
    }
}

// Usage pattern
const rollbackManager = new RollbackManager();

// Before each phase
rollbackManager.createCheckpoint('phase1-start', 'Before AudioContext migration');

// After each critical change
rollbackManager.createCheckpoint('audio-singleton', 'AudioContext singleton implemented');

// If something breaks
if (criticalError) {
    await rollbackManager.rollback('phase1-start');
}
```

## ⚡ PERFORMANCE OPTIMIZATION PATTERNS

### **WebGL + WebAudio Coordination**
```javascript
// Synchronized rendering and audio
class PerformanceCoordinator {
    constructor() {
        this.audioScheduler = new AudioScheduler();
        this.renderScheduler = new RenderScheduler();
        this.performanceProfile = new PerformanceProfile();
    }
    
    // Coordinate audio and visual timing
    scheduleChordVisualization(chord, timing) {
        // Audio scheduled first for precision
        const audioPromise = this.audioScheduler.schedule(chord, timing);
        
        // Visual synchronized to audio
        const visualPromise = this.renderScheduler.schedule(() => {
            this.createChordVisual(chord);
        }, timing - this.getVisualLeadTime());
        
        return Promise.all([audioPromise, visualPromise]);
    }
    
    // Adaptive quality based on performance
    adaptQuality() {
        const performance = this.performanceProfile.getMetrics();
        
        if (performance.frameRate < 45) {
            // Reduce visual quality
            this.renderScheduler.setQuality('medium');
            this.audioScheduler.reduceSampleRate();
        } else if (performance.frameRate > 55) {
            // Restore quality
            this.renderScheduler.setQuality('high');
            this.audioScheduler.restoreSampleRate();
        }
    }
}
```

## 3) Starter code (drop into `src/core/`)

**AudioContextManager.ts**

```ts
// src/core/AudioContextManager.ts
import * as Tone from 'tone';

export class AudioContextManager {
  private static _instance: AudioContextManager | null = null;
  private _context: AudioContext | null = null;
  private _initialized = false;
  private _pending: Array<() => void> = [];

  private constructor() {}

  static getInstance(): AudioContextManager {
    if (!AudioContextManager._instance) {
      AudioContextManager._instance = new AudioContextManager();
    }
    return AudioContextManager._instance;
  }

  get context(): AudioContext | null {
    return this._context;
  }

  async initialize(): Promise<AudioContext> {
    if (this._initialized && this._context) return this._context;
    // Suspend other known contexts if possible (best-effort)
    try {
      // Create master context
      this._context = new (window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext)();
      // If Tone.js present, instruct it to use our context
      try {
        // @ts-ignore
        if (Tone && typeof (Tone as any).setContext === 'function') {
          // Tone v14: set global context
          (Tone as any).setContext(this._context);
        } else if (Tone && (Tone as any).context) {
          (Tone as any).context = this._context;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Could not set Tone context automatically', e);
      }
      this._initialized = true;
      // flush pending ops
      this._pending.forEach(fn => fn());
      this._pending = [];
      return this._context;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('AudioContext initialize failed', err);
      throw err;
    }
  }

  ensure<T>(fn: (ctx: AudioContext) => T): T | Promise<T> {
    if (this._initialized && this._context) {
      return fn(this._context);
    }
    // queue until initialized
    return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
      this._pending.push(async () => {
        try {
          // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-non-null-assertion
          const res = fn(this._context!);
          resolve(res as T);
        } catch (e) {
          reject(e);
        }
      });
    });
  }

  // call on a user gesture to ensure audio resumes (Chrome requires gesture)
  async unlockOnGesture(): Promise<void> {
    const resume = async () => {
      if (!this._context) await this.initialize();
      if (this._context && this._context.state !== 'running') {
        try { await this._context.resume(); } catch {}
      }
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', resume);
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', resume);
    };
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', resume, { once: true });
    window.addEventListener('keydown', resume, { once: true });
  }
}
export default AudioContextManager;
```

**ResourceManager.ts**

```ts
// src/core/ResourceManager.ts
import * as THREE from 'three';

type Disposable = THREE.BufferGeometry | THREE.Material | THREE.Texture | THREE.Object3D;

export class ResourceManager {
  private textures = new Map<string, THREE.Texture>();
  private geometries = new Map<string, THREE.BufferGeometry>();
  private materials = new Map<string, THREE.Material>();
  private tracked = new Set<Disposable>();

  getTexture(key: string, factory: () => THREE.Texture): THREE.Texture {
    if (this.textures.has(key)) return this.textures.get(key)!;
    const t = factory();
    t.needsUpdate = true;
    this.textures.set(key, t);
    this.track(t);
    return t;
  }

  getGeometry(key: string, factory: () => THREE.BufferGeometry): THREE.BufferGeometry {
    if (this.geometries.has(key)) return this.geometries.get(key)!;
    const g = factory();
    this.geometries.set(key, g);
    this.track(g);
    return g;
  }

  getMaterial(key: string, factory: () => THREE.Material): THREE.Material {
    if (this.materials.has(key)) return this.materials.get(key)!;
    const m = factory();
    this.materials.set(key, m);
    this.track(m);
    return m;
  }

  track(resource: Disposable) {
    this.tracked.add(resource);
  }

  disposeResource(resource: Disposable) {
    if (!resource) return;
    // geometry
    // @ts-ignore
    if (resource.dispose) {
      try { (resource as any).dispose(); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
    }
    this.tracked.delete(resource);
  }

  disposeAll(renderer?: THREE.WebGLRenderer) {
    this.tracked.forEach(r => {
      try { (r as any).dispose?.(); } catch {}
    });
    this.tracked.clear();
    this.textures.clear();
    this.geometries.clear();
    this.materials.clear();
    if (renderer) {
      try { renderer.dispose(); } catch {}
    }
  }
}
export default ResourceManager;
```

> **Note:** These files are starter code — the engineer must wire them into the app and replace `new AudioContext()` / direct texture creation calls. Use them as the canonical single source for audio & resources.

## 4) Playwright stress-test script (dev-tools/stress-test.js)

> This is intentionally defensive: it tries to call common programmatic entrypoints (window\.ChordCubes) and falls back to clicking UI elements. **Adapt selectors or function names to match your app.**

```js
// dev-tools/stress-test.js
const fs = require('fs').promises;
const { chromium } = require('playwright');

const URL = process.argv[2] || 'http://localhost:3000'; // change if needed
const RUN_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 minutes
const INTERVAL_MS = 100; // trigger every 100ms (10Hz)

(async () => {
  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 800 } });
  await page.goto(URL, { waitUntil: 'networkidle' });

  console.log('Loaded page', URL);
  await page.waitForTimeout(1000);

  const results = [];
  const start = Date.now();
  while (Date.now() - start < RUN_MS) {
    // Sample memory (if available)
    const mem = await page.evaluate(() => (performance as any).memory ? (performance as any).memory.usedJSHeapSize : null);
    // Try programmatic trigger
    const played = await page.evaluate(() => {
      try {
        const ns = (window as any).ChordCubes;
        if (ns && ns.Application && ns.Application.audioEngine && typeof ns.Application.audioEngine.playChord === 'function') {
          ns.Application.audioEngine.playChord('Cmaj');
          return 'played_api';
        }
        // fallback: click a UI element with data-chord attr
        const btn = document.querySelector('[data-chord]');
        if (btn) { (btn as HTMLElement).click(); return 'clicked'; }
      } catch (e) { return 'error:' + e.message; }
      return 'no-op';
    });

    results.push({ t: Date.now(), mem, played });
    await page.waitForTimeout(INTERVAL_MS);
  }

  await fs.writeFile('stress-results.json', JSON.stringify(results, null, 2));
  await browser.close();
  console.log('Stress test complete. Results saved to stress-results.json');
})();
```

**How to run**

```bash
npm i playwright
node dev-tools/stress-test.js http://localhost:3000
```

```javascript
class MemoryLeakDetector {
    async detectLeak(operation, iterations = 5) {
        const measurements = [];
        
        // Warm up V8 optimizations
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            await operation();
            await this.forceGC();
        }
        
        // Measure retained memory
        for (let i = 0; i < iterations; i++) {
            await this.forceGC();
            const before = performance.memory.usedJSHeapSize;
            
            await operation();
            
            await this.forceGC();
            const after = performance.memory.usedJSHeapSize;
            
            measurements.push(after - before);
        }
        
        // Check for linear growth (leak indicator)
        return this.detectLinearGrowth(measurements);
    }
}
```

```javascript
            Vex.Flow.Renderer.Backends.SVG
        );
        
        this.renderers.set(elementId, renderer);
        this.trackSVGElements(elementId);
        return renderer;
    }
    
    dispose() {
        // Claude's VexFlow cleanup
        this.svgElements.forEach(el => el.remove());
        this.renderers.clear();
    }
}
```

```javascript
        if (window.AudioContext.prototype.constructor.name) count++;
        if (Tone?.context) count++;
```

```javascript
// COMPLETE AudioContext Manager with ALL edge cases handled
class UnifiedAudioContextManager {
    constructor() {
        // Singleton enforcement
        if (UnifiedAudioContextManager._instance) {
            return UnifiedAudioContextManager._instance;
        }
        
        this._initPromise = null;
        this._context = null;
        this._state = 'uninitialized';
        this._suspendedContexts = new Map();
        this._toneContext = null;
        this._webAudioFontContext = null;
        this._disposed = false;
        
        // Track all audio nodes for cleanup
        this._activeNodes = new Set();
        this._scheduledEvents = new Map();
        
        UnifiedAudioContextManager._instance = this;
    }
    
    async initialize() {
        // Guard against multiple initialization
        if (this._disposed) {
            throw new Error('AudioContextManager has been disposed');
        }
        
        if (this._initPromise) {
            return this._initPromise;
        }
        
        this._initPromise = this._performInitialization();
        return this._initPromise;
    }
    
    async _performInitialization() {
        try {
            this._state = 'initializing';
            
            // Step 1: Capture ALL existing contexts
            const existingContexts = this._findAllAudioContexts();
            console.log(`Found ${existingContexts.length} existing AudioContexts`);
            
            // Step 2: Suspend them gracefully
            for (const ctx of existingContexts) {
                if (ctx && ctx.state === 'running') {
                    await this._suspendContext(ctx);
                }
            }
            
            // Step 3: Create unified context with proper configuration
            this._context = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)({
                latencyHint: 'interactive',
                sampleRate: 44100
            });
            
            // Step 4: Handle Safari's user gesture requirement
            if (this._context.state === 'suspended') {
                await this._setupUserGestureResume();
            }
            
            // Step 5: Migrate Tone.js
            await this._migrateToneJS();
            
            // Step 6: Migrate WebAudioFont
            await this._migrateWebAudioFont();
            
            // Step 7: Setup monitoring
            this._setupContextMonitoring();
            
            this._state = 'ready';
            console.log('AudioContext unified successfully');
            
            return this._context;
            
        } catch (error) {
            this._state = 'error';
            this._initPromise = null; // Allow retry
            throw new Error(`AudioContext initialization failed: ${error.message}`);
        }
    }
    
    _findAllAudioContexts() {
        const contexts = [];
        
        // Check all known global references
        const globals = [
            window.audioCtx,
            window.audioContext,
            window.audio_context,
            Tone?.context?._context || Tone?.context,
            window.WebAudioFontPlayer?.context
        ];
        
        globals.forEach(ctx => {
            if (ctx && ctx instanceof (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)) {
                contexts.push(ctx);
            }
        });
        
        // Scan for hidden contexts in libraries
        if (window.WebAudioFontPlayer) {
            try {
                const wafContext = new WebAudioFontPlayer().context;
                if (wafContext) contexts.push(wafContext);
            } catch (e) {}
        }
        
        // Remove duplicates
        return [...new Set(contexts)];
    }
    
    async _suspendContext(context) {
        const id = Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
        this._suspendedContexts.set(id, {
            context: context,
            timestamp: Date.now(),
            state: context.state
        });
        
        if (context.suspend) {
            await context.suspend();
        }
    }
    
    async _migrateToneJS() {
        if (!window.Tone) return;
        
        console.log('Migrating Tone.js to unified context...');
        
        // Store existing Tone state
        const toneState = {
            transport: Tone.Transport.state,
            bpm: Tone.Transport.bpm.value,
            position: Tone.Transport.position
        };
        
        // Stop transport
        if (Tone.Transport.state === 'started') {
            Tone.Transport.stop();
        }
        
        // Dispose old context properly
        if (Tone.context && Tone.context.dispose) {
            await Tone.context.dispose();
        }
        
        // Create new Tone context with our AudioContext
        Tone.setContext(new Tone.Context({
            context: this._context,
            lookAhead: 0.1,
            updateInterval: 0.05
        }));
        
        // Restore state
        Tone.Transport.bpm.value = toneState.bpm;
        
        this._toneContext = Tone.context;
    }
    
    async _migrateWebAudioFont() {
        if (!window.WebAudioFontPlayer) return;
        
        console.log('Migrating WebAudioFont to unified context...');
        
        // WebAudioFont is tricky - it creates contexts internally
        // We need to monkey-patch its constructor
        const OriginalPlayer = window.WebAudioFontPlayer;
        
        window.WebAudioFontPlayer = function() {
            const player = new OriginalPlayer();
            // Force it to use our context
            Object.defineProperty(player, 'context', {
                get: () => UnifiedAudioContextManager._instance._context,
                set: () => {} // Ignore attempts to set
            });
            return player;
        };
        
        // Copy prototype
        window.WebAudioFontPlayer.prototype = OriginalPlayer.prototype;
    }
    
    _setupUserGestureResume() {
        return new Promise((resolve) => {
            const resumeContext = async () => {
                if (this._context.state === 'suspended') {
                    await this._context.resume();
                }
                document.removeEventListener('click', resumeContext);
                document.removeEventListener('keydown', resumeContext);
                resolve();
            };
            
            document.addEventListener('click', resumeContext);
            document.addEventListener('keydown', resumeContext);
            
            // Show user prompt if needed
            if (this._context.state === 'suspended') {
                this._showAudioPrompt();
            }
        });
    }
    
    _setupContextMonitoring() {
        // Monitor for context state changes
        this._context.onstatechange = () => {
            console.log(`AudioContext state: ${this._context.state}`);
            
            if (this._context.state === 'interrupted') {
                // Handle iOS interruption
                this._handleInterruption();
            }
        };
        
        // Monitor performance
        setInterval(() => {
            if (this._context.state === 'running') {
                const latency = this._context.baseLatency + this._context.outputLatency;
                if (latency > 0.1) {
                    console.warn(`High audio latency detected: ${latency}s`);
                }
            }
        }, 1000);
    }
    
    // Safe node creation with tracking
    createNode(type, ...args) {
        if (!this._context) {
            throw new Error('AudioContext not initialized');
        }
        
        const node = this._context[`create${type}`](...args);
        this._activeNodes.add(node);
        
        // Auto-cleanup when node is garbage collected
        const weakRef = new WeakRef(node);
        this._scheduleNodeCleanup(weakRef);
        
        return node;
    }
    
    // Complete disposal
    async dispose() {
        if (this._disposed) return;
        
        this._disposed = true;
        this._state = 'disposing';
        
        // Stop all scheduled events
        this._scheduledEvents.forEach(event => clearTimeout(event));
        
        // Disconnect all nodes
        this._activeNodes.forEach(node => {
            try {
                node.disconnect();
            } catch (e) {}
        });
        
        // Close context
        if (this._context && this._context.close) {
            await this._context.close();
        }
        
        // Clear singleton
        UnifiedAudioContextManager._instance = null;
    }
}
```

#### **Day 1 Afternoon: WebMIDI Integration Fix**

```javascript
class SafeWebMIDIManager {
    constructor(audioManager) {
        this.audioManager = audioManager;
        this.midiAccess = null;
        this.inputs = new Map();
        this.outputs = new Map();
        this.listeners = new Map();
        this.disposed = false;
    }
    
    async initialize() {
        if (this.disposed) throw new Error('MIDI Manager disposed');
        
        try {
            this.midiAccess = await navigator.requestMIDIAccess({
                sysex: false // Don't request sysex unless needed
            });
            
            // Setup port monitoring
            this.midiAccess.onstatechange = (e) => this.handlePortChange(e);
            
            // Initialize all ports
            this.scanPorts();
            
            return true;
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('MIDI initialization failed:', error);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    scanPorts() {
        // Clear existing
        this.cleanup();
        
        // Setup inputs
        this.midiAccess.inputs.forEach((input, id) => {
            this.setupInput(id, input);
        });
        
        // Setup outputs
        this.midiAccess.outputs.forEach((output, id) => {
            this.outputs.set(id, output);
        });
    }
    
    setupInput(id, input) {
        // Create handler with proper cleanup tracking
        const handler = (midiEvent) => {
            this.handleMIDIMessage(midiEvent);
        };
        
        // Store for cleanup
        this.listeners.set(id, handler);
        this.inputs.set(id, input);
        
        // Attach listener
        input.onmidimessage = handler;
    }
    
    handleMIDIMessage(event) {
        const [status, note, velocity] = event.data;
        
        // Parse MIDI message
        const channel = status & 0xF;
        const command = status >> 4;
        
        // Route to appropriate handler
        switch (command) {
            case 0x9: // Note On
                this.handleNoteOn(channel, note, velocity);
                break;
            case 0x8: // Note Off
                this.handleNoteOff(channel, note);
                break;
            case 0xB: // Control Change
                this.handleControlChange(channel, note, velocity);
                break;
        }
    }
    
    cleanup() {
        // Remove all listeners
        this.listeners.forEach((handler, id) => {
            const input = this.inputs.get(id);
            if (input) {
                input.onmidimessage = null;
                if (input.close) input.close();
            }
        });
        
        // Close outputs
        this.outputs.forEach(output => {
            if (output.close) output.close();
        });
        
        this.listeners.clear();
        this.inputs.clear();
        this.outputs.clear();
    }
    
    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;
        
        this.disposed = true;
        this.cleanup();
        
        if (this.midiAccess) {
            this.midiAccess.onstatechange = null;
        }
    }
}
```

#### **Day 3: Three.js Resource Manager**

```javascript
class ThreeJSResourceManager {
    constructor() {
        this.textures = new Map();
        this.geometries = new Map();
        this.materials = new Map();
        this.meshes = new WeakMap();
        
        // GPU memory tracking
        this.gpuMemoryUsed = 0;
        this.gpuMemoryLimit = 512 * 1024 * 1024; // 512MB default
        
        // LRU cache for textures
        this.textureAccessOrder = [];
        
        // Setup monitoring
        this.setupMemoryMonitoring();
    }
    
    setupMemoryMonitoring() {
        // Check for WebGL memory extension
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        const gl = canvas.getContext('webgl2') || canvas.getContext('webgl');
        
        if (gl) {
            const ext = gl.getExtension('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');
            if (ext) {
                const renderer = gl.getParameter(ext.UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL);
                console.log('GPU:', renderer);
                
                // Adjust memory limit based on GPU
                if (renderer.includes('Intel')) {
                    this.gpuMemoryLimit = 256 * 1024 * 1024; // Lower for integrated
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    getTexture(key, creator) {
        // Check cache first
        if (this.textures.has(key)) {
            this.updateTextureAccess(key);
            return this.textures.get(key);
        }
        
        // Check memory pressure
        if (this.gpuMemoryUsed > this.gpuMemoryLimit * 0.8) {
            this.evictLRUTextures();
        }
        
        // Create new texture
        const texture = creator();
        
        // Estimate memory usage
        const memoryUsage = this.estimateTextureMemory(texture);
        
        // Store with metadata
        this.textures.set(key, texture);
        this.textureAccessOrder.push(key);
        this.gpuMemoryUsed += memoryUsage;
        
        // Setup disposal tracking
        texture.addEventListener('dispose', () => {
            this.handleTextureDisposal(key, memoryUsage);
        });
        
        return texture;
    }
    
    estimateTextureMemory(texture) {
        if (!texture.image) return 0;
        
        const width = texture.image.width || 512;
        const height = texture.image.height || 512;
        const bytesPerPixel = 4; // RGBA
        const mipmaps = texture.generateMipmaps ? 1.33 : 1; // Mipmap overhead
        
        return width * height * bytesPerPixel * mipmaps;
    }
    
    evictLRUTextures() {
        const evictCount = Math.floor(this.textureAccessOrder.length * 0.2); // Evict 20%
        
        for (let i = 0; i < evictCount; i++) {
            const key = this.textureAccessOrder.shift();
            const texture = this.textures.get(key);
            
            if (texture) {
                texture.dispose();
                this.textures.delete(key);
            }
        }
        
        console.log(`Evicted ${evictCount} textures for memory pressure`);
    }
    
    createManagedMesh(geometry, material) {
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
        
        // Track mesh resources
        this.meshes.set(mesh, {
            geometry: geometry,
            material: material,
            created: Date.now()
        });
        
        return mesh;
    }
    
    disposeMesh(mesh) {
        const resources = this.meshes.get(mesh);
        
        if (resources) {
            // Dispose geometry
            if (resources.geometry && resources.geometry.dispose) {
                resources.geometry.dispose();
            }
            
            // Dispose material(s)
            if (resources.material) {
                if (Array.isArray(resources.material)) {
                    resources.material.forEach(mat => {
                        if (mat.map) mat.map.dispose();
                        if (mat.dispose) mat.dispose();
                    });
                } else {
                    if (resources.material.map) resources.material.map.dispose();
                    if (resources.material.dispose) resources.material.dispose();
                }
            }
            
            this.meshes.delete(mesh);
        }
    }
    
    // Complete cleanup
    disposeAll() {
        // Dispose all textures
        this.textures.forEach(texture => texture.dispose());
        this.textures.clear();
        
        // Dispose all geometries
        this.geometries.forEach(geometry => geometry.dispose());
        this.geometries.clear();
        
        // Dispose all materials
        this.materials.forEach(material => material.dispose());
        this.materials.clear();
        
        this.gpuMemoryUsed = 0;
    }
}
```

#### **Day 4: VexFlow Resource Management**

```javascript
class VexFlowResourceManager {
    constructor() {
        this.renderers = new Map();
        this.contexts = new Map();
        this.svgElements = new Map();
        this.disposed = false;
    }
    
    createRenderer(containerId, width = 800, height = 200) {
        if (this.disposed) throw new Error('VexFlow manager disposed');
        
        // Clean up existing renderer if present
        if (this.renderers.has(containerId)) {
            this.disposeRenderer(containerId);
        }
        
        const container = document.getElementById(containerId);
        if (!container) throw new Error(`Container ${containerId} not found`);
        
        // Create renderer
        const renderer = new Vex.Flow.Renderer(
            container,
            Vex.Flow.Renderer.Backends.SVG
        );
        
        renderer.resize(width, height);
        const context = renderer.getContext();
        
        // Track SVG element for cleanup
        const svgElement = container.querySelector('svg');
        
        // Store references
        this.renderers.set(containerId, renderer);
        this.contexts.set(containerId, context);
        this.svgElements.set(containerId, svgElement);
        
        // Setup mutation observer for leak detection
        this.setupLeakDetection(containerId, svgElement);
        
        return { renderer, context };
    }
    
    setupLeakDetection(containerId, svgElement) {
        const observer = new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
            // Check for excessive DOM nodes
            const nodeCount = svgElement.querySelectorAll('*').length;
            if (nodeCount > 1000) {
                console.warn(`VexFlow SVG has ${nodeCount} nodes - potential leak`);
                this.cleanupExcessiveNodes(svgElement);
            }
        });
        
        observer.observe(svgElement, {
            childList: true,
            subtree: true
        });
        
        // Store observer for cleanup
        svgElement._vexflowObserver = observer;
    }
    
    cleanupExcessiveNodes(svgElement) {
        // Remove old staff renderings
        const groups = svgElement.querySelectorAll('g');
        if (groups.length > 10) {
            // Keep only last 10 renderings
            for (let i = 0; i < groups.length - 10; i++) {
                groups[i].remove();
            }
        }
    }
    
    disposeRenderer(containerId) {
        // Get references
        const renderer = this.renderers.get(containerId);
        const context = this.contexts.get(containerId);
        const svgElement = this.svgElements.get(containerId);
        
        // Cleanup SVG observer
        if (svgElement && svgElement._vexflowObserver) {
            svgElement._vexflowObserver.disconnect();
        }
        
        // Remove SVG element
        if (svgElement && svgElement.parentNode) {
            svgElement.parentNode.removeChild(svgElement);
        }
        
        // Clear maps
        this.renderers.delete(containerId);
        this.contexts.delete(containerId);
        this.svgElements.delete(containerId);
    }
    
    disposeAll() {
        if (this.disposed) return;
        
        // Dispose all renderers
        Array.from(this.renderers.keys()).forEach(id => {
            this.disposeRenderer(id);
        });
        
        this.disposed = true;
    }
}
```

### **DAYS 5-6: PERFORMANCE OPTIMIZATION**

#### **Day 5: Spatial Hashing (FIXED)**

```javascript
class SpatialHashGrid {
    constructor(cellSize = 100) {
        this.cellSize = cellSize;
        this.grid = new Map();
        this.objectCells = new WeakMap();
    }
    
    // Fixed hash function that handles negative coordinates
    hashKey(x, y, z = 0) {
        const cellX = Math.floor(x / this.cellSize);
        const cellY = Math.floor(y / this.cellSize);
        const cellZ = Math.floor(z / this.cellSize);
        
        // Use a proper separator that won't conflict
        return `${cellX}|${cellY}|${cellZ}`;
    }
    
    insert(object) {
        const pos = object.position || object;
        const key = this.hashKey(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z || 0);
        
        if (!this.grid.has(key)) {
            this.grid.set(key, new Set());
        }
        
        this.grid.get(key).add(object);
        this.objectCells.set(object, key);
    }
    
    remove(object) {
        const key = this.objectCells.get(object);
        if (key && this.grid.has(key)) {
            this.grid.get(key).delete(object);
            if (this.grid.get(key).size === 0) {
                this.grid.delete(key);
            }
        }
        this.objectCells.delete(object);
    }
    
    update(object) {
        const oldKey = this.objectCells.get(object);
        const pos = object.position || object;
        const newKey = this.hashKey(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z || 0);
        
        if (oldKey !== newKey) {
            this.remove(object);
            this.insert(object);
        }
    }
    
    getNearby(position, radius = 1) {
        const nearby = new Set();
        const pos = position.position || position;
        
        // Calculate cell range to check
        const cellRadius = Math.ceil(radius / this.cellSize);
        
        for (let dx = -cellRadius; dx <= cellRadius; dx++) {
            for (let dy = -cellRadius; dy <= cellRadius; dy++) {
                for (let dz = -cellRadius; dz <= cellRadius; dz++) {
                    const checkX = pos.x + (dx * this.cellSize);
                    const checkY = pos.y + (dy * this.cellSize);
                    const checkZ = (pos.z || 0) + (dz * this.cellSize);
                    
                    const key = this.hashKey(checkX, checkY, checkZ);
                    
                    if (this.grid.has(key)) {
                        this.grid.get(key).forEach(obj => nearby.add(obj));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        return Array.from(nearby);
    }
    
    clear() {
        this.grid.clear();
        // WeakMap will auto-cleanup
    }
}
```

#### **Day 6: Render Loop Optimization (FIXED)**

```javascript
class OptimizedRenderLoop {
    constructor(renderer, scene, camera) {
        this.renderer = renderer;
        this.scene = scene;
        this.camera = camera;
        
        this.spatialHash = new SpatialHashGrid();
        this.frameTime = 16.67; // Target 60fps
        this.lastFrameTime = 0;
        this.deltaTime = 0;
        
        // Performance monitoring
        this.frameCount = 0;
        this.fpsHistory = [];
        this.performanceMode = 'high'; // high, medium, low
        
        // Render optimizations
        this.frustumCuller = new THREE.Frustum();
        this.cameraMatrix = new THREE.Matrix4();
        
        // Object pools
        this.vector3Pool = [];
        this.quaternionPool = [];
        
        this.running = false;
    }
    
    start() {
        if (this.running) return;
        this.running = true;
        this.lastFrameTime = performance.now();
        this.animate();
    }
    
    stop() {
        this.running = false;
    }
    
    animate = () => {
        if (!this.running) return;
        
        const currentTime = performance.now();
        this.deltaTime = currentTime - this.lastFrameTime;
        
        // FPS monitoring
        this.updateFPS();
        
        // Adaptive quality
        this.updatePerformanceMode();
        
        // Only update physics at fixed timestep
        if (this.deltaTime >= this.frameTime) {
            this.updatePhysics(this.deltaTime / 1000);
            this.lastFrameTime = currentTime;
        }
        
        // Always render for smooth visuals
        this.render();
        
        requestAnimationFrame(this.animate);
    }
    
    updatePhysics(dt) {
        // Rebuild spatial hash
        this.spatialHash.clear();
        
        // Update all cubes
        this.scene.traverse((child) => {
            if (child.userData.cube) {
                // Update position
                if (child.userData.velocity) {
                    child.position.add(
                        child.userData.velocity.clone().multiplyScalar(dt)
                    );
                }
                
                // Add to spatial hash
                this.spatialHash.insert(child);
            }
        });
        
        // Collision detection (optimized)
        this.checkCollisions();
    }
    
    checkCollisions() {
        const checked = new Set();
        
        this.scene.traverse((child) => {
            if (!child.userData.cube) return;
            
            const nearby = this.spatialHash.getNearby(child.position, 2);
            
            nearby.forEach(other => {
                if (child === other) return;
                
                // Create unique pair key to avoid double-checking
                const pairKey = [child.uuid, other.uuid].sort().join('-');
                if (checked.has(pairKey)) return;
                checked.add(pairKey);
                
                if (this.checkCollision(child, other)) {
                    this.handleCollision(child, other);
                }
            });
        });
    }
    
    checkCollision(obj1, obj2) {
        const dist = obj1.position.distanceTo(obj2.position);
        return dist < 2; // Assuming cube size of 1
    }
    
    handleCollision(obj1, obj2) {
        // Swap velocities (simple elastic collision)
        if (obj1.userData.velocity && obj2.userData.velocity) {
            const temp = obj1.userData.velocity.clone();
            obj1.userData.velocity = obj2.userData.velocity.clone();
            obj2.userData.velocity = temp;
        }
    }
    
    render() {
        // Frustum culling
        this.cameraMatrix.multiplyMatrices(
            this.camera.projectionMatrix,
            this.camera.matrixWorldInverse
        );
        this.frustumCuller.setFromProjectionMatrix(this.cameraMatrix);
        
        // Hide objects outside frustum
        this.scene.traverse((child) => {
            if (child.isMesh) {
                child.visible = this.frustumCuller.intersectsObject(child);
            }
        });
        
        // Render based on performance mode
        switch (this.performanceMode) {
            case 'low':
                this.renderer.setPixelRatio(0.5);
                break;
            case 'medium':
                this.renderer.setPixelRatio(1);
                break;
            case 'high':
                this.renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio);
                break;
        }
        
        this.renderer.render(this.scene, this.camera);
    }
    
    updateFPS() {
        this.frameCount++;
        const fps = 1000 / this.deltaTime;
        this.fpsHistory.push(fps);
        
        // Keep only last 60 frames
        if (this.fpsHistory.length > 60) {
            this.fpsHistory.shift();
        }
    }
    
    updatePerformanceMode() {
        const avgFPS = this.fpsHistory.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / this.fpsHistory.length;
        
        if (avgFPS < 30 && this.performanceMode !== 'low') {
            this.performanceMode = 'low';
            console.log('Switching to low performance mode');
        } else if (avgFPS < 50 && this.performanceMode === 'high') {
            this.performanceMode = 'medium';
            console.log('Switching to medium performance mode');
        } else if (avgFPS > 55 && this.performanceMode !== 'high') {
            this.performanceMode = 'high';
            console.log('Switching to high performance mode');
        }
    }
    
    dispose() {
        this.stop();
        this.spatialHash.clear();
        this.fpsHistory = [];
    }
}
```

### **DAYS 7-8: MODULARIZATION**

#### **Day 7: Phoenix Hub Integration Wrapper**

```javascript
class ChordCubesPhoenixModule {
    constructor() {
        this.initialized = false;
        this.mounted = false;
        
        // Core systems
        this.audioManager = null;
        this.resourceManager = null;
        this.renderLoop = null;
        this.midiManager = null;
        this.vexflowManager = null;
        
        // Feature flags for gradual rollout
        this.features = {
            unifiedAudio: false,
            resourceManagement: false,
            optimizedRender: false,
            phoenixIntegration: false
        };
        
        // Error tracking
        this.errors = [];
    }
    
    async initialize() {
        if (this.initialized) return;
        
        try {
            // Load feature flags from config
            await this.loadFeatureFlags();
            
            // Initialize based on flags
            if (this.features.unifiedAudio) {
                this.audioManager = new UnifiedAudioContextManager();
                await this.audioManager.initialize();
            }
            
            if (this.features.resourceManagement) {
                this.resourceManager = new ThreeJSResourceManager();
                this.vexflowManager = new VexFlowResourceManager();
            }
            
            // MIDI is always needed for this app
            this.midiManager = new SafeWebMIDIManager(this.audioManager);
            await this.midiManager.initialize();
            
            this.initialized = true;
            
        } catch (error) {
            this.handleError('Initialization failed', error);
            throw error;
        }
    }
    
    async mount(container) {
        if (!this.initialized) {
            await this.initialize();
        }
        
        if (this.mounted) {
            throw new Error('Already mounted');
        }
        
        try {
            // Setup render target
            this.container = container;
            
            // Initialize Three.js scene
            await this.setupScene();
            
            // Start render loop if enabled
            if (this.features.optimizedRender && this.renderLoop) {
                this.renderLoop.start();
            } else {
                // Use legacy render loop
                this.startLegacyRender();
            }
            
            // Setup event handlers
            this.setupEventHandlers();
            
            this.mounted = true;
            
            // Notify Phoenix Hub
            this.notifyPhoenixHub('mounted');
            
        } catch (error) {
            this.handleError('Mount failed', error);
            await this.unmount();
            throw error;
        }
    }
    
    async unmount() {
        if (!this.mounted) return;
        
        try {
            // Stop rendering
            if (this.renderLoop) {
                this.renderLoop.stop();
            }
            
            // Cleanup resources
            if (this.resourceManager) {
                this.resourceManager.disposeAll();
            }
            
            if (this.vexflowManager) {
                this.vexflowManager.disposeAll();
            }
            
            // Cleanup MIDI
            if (this.midiManager) {
                this.midiManager.dispose();
            }
            
            // Cleanup audio last
            if (this.audioManager) {
                await this.audioManager.dispose();
            }
            
            // Clear container
            if (this.container) {
                this.container.innerHTML = '';
            }
            
            this.mounted = false;
            
            // Notify Phoenix Hub
            this.notifyPhoenixHub('unmounted');
            
        } catch (error) {
            this.handleError('Unmount failed', error);
        }
    }
    
    // Phoenix Hub message handler
    handleMessage(message) {
        switch (message.type) {
            case 'PLAY_CHORD':
                return this.playChord(message.data);
                
            case 'SET_TEMPO':
                return this.setTempo(message.data);
                
            case 'GET_STATE':
                return this.getState();
                
            case 'ENABLE_FEATURE':
                return this.enableFeature(message.data);
                
            case 'HEALTH_CHECK':
                return this.healthCheck();
                
            default:
                console.warn('Unknown message type:', message.type);
        }
    }
    
    async healthCheck() {
        const checks = {
            audio: await this.checkAudio(),
            rendering: this.checkRendering(),
            memory: this.checkMemory(),
            errors: this.errors.slice(-10) // Last 10 errors
        };
        
        return {
            healthy: checks.audio && checks.rendering && checks.memory,
            details: checks
        };
    }
    
    handleError(context, error) {
        const errorInfo = {
            timestamp: Date.now(),
            context,
            message: error.message,
            stack: error.stack
        };
        
        this.errors.push(errorInfo);
        
        // Report to monitoring
        if (window.errorReporter) {
            window.errorReporter.report(errorInfo);
        }
        
        console.error(`[ChordCubes] ${context}:`, error);
    }
}
```
